# Batch Tracker test matrix

Each row is one test case. COVERED means I read the test body and it asserts the expected result. GAP means no such test exists; a name after Nearest is the closest test and what it misses. Counts below come from the rows. Requirement None marks setup checks and behaviour with no requirement ID yet. IDs of removed rows stay unused, and new rows continue the sequence inside their area. A category with no possible case sits in No case. Open product questions sit in Unspecified and have no expected result.

## No case

| Subject | Category | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GET /api/me | Edge, Corner | Route takes only the token. Token cases sit in Auth and tokens. |
| GET /healthz, /api/healthz | Edge, Corner | No input and no state: it answers or the process is gone. Process loss sits in Service failures. A flood is its Negative case, in Rate limits and retries. |
| Worker GET /healthz | Edge, Corner | No input and no state. A flood is its Negative case, in Rate limits and retries. |
| Live GET /healthz | Edge, Corner | No input and no state. A flood is its Negative case, in Rate limits and retries. |
| Line GET /healthz | Edge, Corner | No input and no state. A flood is its Negative case, in Rate limits and retries. |
| Dispatcher GET /healthz | Corner | No input and no state. A flood is its Negative case, in Rate limits and retries. |
| Monitor GET /alive | Edge, Corner | No input and no state. A flood is its Negative case, in Rate limits and retries. |
| Closed and Rejected as a transition | Happy | A final state has no step out, so nothing can succeed. |
| Not found page | Negative | The page has no action to refuse. |
| Change feed, operation banner, pod network, Logs, Performance | Chaos and load Happy | A failure run has no happy path. |
| Change feed, operation banner, pod network, Logs, Performance | Architecture | No structure rule attaches to these requirements. |
| Cookie session | Architecture Edge, Corner | A source rule passes or fails, with nothing between. |
| Cookie session | Chaos and load Happy, Edge | A failure run has no happy path, and the session holds no limit to reach. |
| Route enumeration | Web component, E2E, Chaos and load | A build rule, no page and no runtime load. |
| Delayed responses | E2E, Chaos and load | REQ-BT-049 names unit, integration and smoke. |
| Secrets and logs, Injection, Privileges | Paired cases | Each row is one attack or one check, with no paired case of its own. |

## Built, owner has not ruled

Docs or code settle these now, so they left Unspecified. No owner ruling stands behind any of them. IDs stay as they were.

| ID | Question | What stands | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| U-51 | What a valid X-Correlation-Id is. | One header matching `[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,64}` is taken. Anything else, two headers included, gets a generated ID and the request goes on. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Correlation |
| U-52 | Where the edge that generates the ID is. | The browser and the Line generate it, and a service generates one for a request that came without. A request with no answer shows the ID the browser sent. Built, the orchestrator decided. | `app/web/src/api/correlation.test.tsx`, `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Correlation |
| U-53 | How traceId and the correlation ID relate. | traceId stays per step, correlationId links the action. It sits on the pending event, not on a step, so the hash chain is unchanged. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Correlation |
| U-54 | One ID per run or per batch for background work. | Sweep, dead letters and a Worker delivery reuse the ID on the event. Reconcile and a dispatcher turn take a new one per run. Nightly uses the ID of its request. The Line takes one per walk. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Correlation |
| U-55 | Which ID a replay keeps. | The ID of the replay request. The old one stays in `pending.replayOf`. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Correlation |
| U-57 | How the Logs filter matches an ID. | Exact match after trimming spaces. | `docs/architecture/events.md`, `app/tests/BatchTracker.UnitTests/CorrelationFlowTests.cs` |
| U-58 | Logs read /line/logs only, and REQ-BT-052 says every service's log lines reach the Logs page. | Built, the orchestrator decided: API, Worker, Dispatcher and Live report warnings and errors to Live, GET /live/logs serves the last 5000 in memory, ops.admin, Line entries stay in /line/logs. The web reads both and lists them together. Service entries are not pushed, so the page says so and reads them at a press. | `app/tests/BatchTracker.IntegrationTests/ServiceLogRouteTests.cs`, `app/web/src/pages/logs/service-logs.test.tsx` |
| U-59 | What an error says about a batch when none is involved. | Built, the orchestrator decided. The API sends "No batch was affected." for routes outside batches and events, "Nothing changed: this was only a read." for a list read. The web shows what the server sent, and with no answer says the server did not say what this did to any batch, or that a read changed nothing. | `app/src/BatchTracker.Infrastructure/ProblemWords.cs`, `app/web/src/pages/problem-words.test.tsx` |
| U-60 | Whether refused pages keep Switch user. | They do not. REQ-BT-055 requires it. The button stays in the header only. | `app/web/src/pages/batch-detail/batch-detail.test.tsx` :: REQ-BT-016 403 on load, another farm, gives a way out |
| U-29 | REQ-BT-049 gives no numbers: first delay, growth, delay cap, count that brings 429, quiet period, Retry-After. | First 2 failures free, then 1 s doubling to 30 s. Count clears after 15 min with no failure. More than 10 at once get 429. From the 10th failure in the window: 429 with Retry-After until the count clears, one wrong sign in is held and counted, a right one passes. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Failed sign-ins |
| U-31 | Services under REQ-BT-049. Owner said unauthenticated requests. Line and Live also answer 401 through the ingress. | Every 401 in API, Live and Line counts and is delayed, except GET /api/me with no credential. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Failed sign-ins |
| U-32 | The count with two API pods. Docs silent. | Counted per pod. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Failed sign-ins |
| U-33 | REQ-BT-049 and Worker routes. It names signed in users and health routes as never delayed, not the internal token. | A valid internal token is never delayed or counted. A bad one counts on Live. The Worker is not in the failure delays. | `docs/architecture/api.md` :: Failed sign-ins |
